## Security recovery and Windows follow-up — 2026-10-24

- The owner authorized breach remediation and antivirus installation, explicitly requiring that no ports close. Root removed the identified backdoor after preserving evidence: killed only the two exact suspended processes, quarantined twelve identified files and masked the exact service/timer; the cron was already quarantined. This does not establish whole-host cleanliness or credential/artifact integrity. Keep signing, publication and owner upgrades held until clean-environment and credential recovery are established. Do not copy signing keys to CI or casually replace Android/update signing identities.
- Installed official Debian ClamAV and auditd. Real EICAR/known-backdoor detection tests passed with a clean control unflagged. Protected, resource-bounded scan/update services serialize engine use; definitions update every four hours, scans run nightly and a persistence/service guard runs each minute. No automatic file deletion or network blocking is configured. The initial bounded scan finishes at11:37:36UTC:9124 files/1640 directories, zero detections and scanner exit0. This is a result for the stated scan scope, not a whole-host clean result. The broader nightly scope was also started manually at11:44:24UTC and remains in progress. Final guard source c86d9fd9e64aaac2331da060e5d8361aa67cc08cda10a74208d6d113cb0a21a4 passes35 focused final controls, with42 earlier checks under their own pins. The duplicate service remains disabled and the new syslog has no skipped-lock flood. Do not describe this as a packaged 1.0.15 installer correction.

## Working conventions

- The application name is OpenWeb PBX and the public repository is `embire2/openwebpbx`.
- Work on the `customization` branch. Keep the original project's license and copyright notices.
- Keep operational `fusionpbx` database, configuration, service, protocol-header, and filesystem identifiers compatible with the installed stack.
- Administration endpoints must retain their existing authentication, permissions, and CSRF checks. The desktop application launcher uses the permission-filtered menu.
- Local checkout: `/root/fusionpbx`. Live application: `/var/www/fusionpbx`. Deploy source changes without copying `.git`, `.env`, browser state, or validation artifacts.
- Optional Windows test credentials are in the local, untracked `.env` file. Never commit, print, or copy credentials into the live web root. Keep that file mode `600`.
- Update this file as work progresses. Record actual verification results and outstanding work without credentials.
- Keep [3CX.md](3CX.md) as the current roadmap toward the user's full 3CX-like PBX goal. Update affected tasks when implementation, verification, deployment or discovered gaps change; include the update with the related work.
- Preserve roadmap task IDs and record completion dates, commit/release references, actual test evidence and remaining limitations. Leave partially implemented or unverified behavior unchecked, and record external blockers without credentials.
- Before a release, reconcile `3CX.md`, `docs/feature-coverage.md` and the relevant operating guides. Historical progress entries below remain a dated record, not the current feature checklist.
- The project homepage source is `website/public/`. Deploy only that static-site allowlist to its own document root, and refresh its release catalogue with verified assets/platform results whenever releases change. cPanel access details stay in the ignored mode-600 `.env`; never put them in website assets or logs.

## Automatic updates and Android usability — 2026-10-09 (released 1.0.4)

- User requested detected releases, optional or required downloads, verified download before shutdown, automatic install/restart and robust recovery. Confirmed Debian 13 only for Linux; Ubuntu is outside this release. Targeting 1.0.4 because 1.0.3 has no automatic update client.
- Implementing signed release metadata pinned in Android and a separate privileged server updater, tenant-scoped Android policies, instance-admin server policies, UTC maintenance windows, active-call deferral and durable recovery. Tenant administrators must not restart a shared instance. Release signing material is private outside source and web roots.
- Android package installation and reopening must respect operating-system permissions and background restrictions; required policy cannot guarantee silent installation on every personal phone. Do not describe the mechanism as foolproof or claim unperformed recovery tests.

- Initial update controls passed 34 real PostgreSQL checks and 20 live HTTPS checks for authorization, tenant isolation, required/optional policies, CSRF, validation, queue deduplication/rate limiting and safe status output. Runtime web changes are deployed after a private database/source backup; the independent update worker and release are still being developed.

The selected restored rule pointed at disabled Vox, while PORTA was registered and up. Added a highest-priority national 0/10-digit route restricted to extension 1000 using PORTA; other users/rules/trunk enablement remain unchanged. The previous routing JSON is backed up privately. User retry/real audio acceptance is pending.

Do not silently replace customer forwarding preferences. ## Immediate call disconnection investigation — 2026-10-09

- The user reported that actual handset calls still beeped and disconnected after the 1.0.4 update. Earlier internal software-phone tests did not qualify this carrier/physical-handset path. Latest real attempts reached the server; both outside calls were answered by PORTA, then ended within a second because the server sent BYE. The internal call to unregistered extension 1001 also answered and ended without voicemail.
- Found two concrete server defects. The installed conversion module and the passthrough G.729 module both autoloaded; after restart the passthrough implementation shadowed the converter, producing a native decoder error and immediately ending the bridge. Disabled the conflicting passthrough module in the persistent server configuration, reloaded only the codec modules with idle-call/pause checks and retained a private configuration backup. Trunks, routes, credentials and other phone settings are unchanged.
- The restored call handler passed unsupported voicemail action `leave`; native voicemail expects `save`. Deployed the corrected caller action while preserving mailbox-login action `check`. Fifty-three runtime checks now include the actual native dispatcher with synthetic I/O, and 149 policy checks passed. Actual isolated voicemail recording verification is underway.
- After the codec repair, an authenticated call through the real enabled PORTA route from extension 1000 to the previously authorized test number answered and sustained 25.1 seconds, with 1,034 sent and 1,249 received RTP packets. The test caller ended the call normally; neither leg logged native engine errors. This is an actual carrier call, while audibility on the customer's physical handset remains awaiting their retry.
- Replaced the audio-readiness module-presence test with codec-inventory validation so simultaneous passthrough/converter registration no longer appears usable. Thirty-one readiness checks passed, including the exact conflict in both orders. Android investigation independently reproduced missing communication-audio mode in the signed 1.0.4 app; a focused dependency/ringtone fix and immutable 1.0.5 packages are in verification. Existing public 1.0.4 assets remain unchanged.

- Final isolated voicemail recording and playback passed: 10.64 seconds / 170,284 bytes, 531 playback RTP packets containing the expected 500 Hz tone, and zero queued email. The agent removed only its test tenant, service, phones, exact contacts, media and CDRs. Candidate Linux migration now normalizes the known codec conflict through `bootstrap.php`, so the installed 1.0.4 helper executes it after private configuration backup; 17 focused migration checks passed. Linux and Windows 1.0.5 native builds are ready; C# updater logic is unchanged.
- Android 1.0.5 passed the missing-audio-mode regression, an outgoing focus/background/media workflow and an incoming ringing/answer/media workflow. The incoming fixture originally used an unauthenticated loopback peer that entered public ingress and correctly returned404; an engine-originated call between the same disposable phones reached the intended tenant route. No customer ACL or phone configuration was changed. Complete source packaging and final fixture cleanup are in progress.

- Final Android 1.0.5 APK and corresponding source are frozen; ten JVM tests, release lint and both native audio methods passed. Source validation covered 38,024 entries, 52 application files and 35 dependency source JARs including AndroidX Media. Disposable contacts, database/media fixtures, emulator processes and four exact test firewall rules were removed; no customer phones or trunks were changed.
